Unlike the original Angstrom, L2 operates entirely on-chain through hooks, leveraging the rollup's priority fee auction mechanism for MEV protection.\n8\t\n9\t## Key Architecture Changes (L2 vs Original)\n10\t\n11\t- **Hook-Only Design**: No off-chain components or custom order types - all functionality through Uniswap V4 hooks\n12\t- **On-Chain ToB Calculation**: Top-of-block bid calculation happens entirely within the hook\n13\t- **Priority Fee MEV Tax**: Protection via `tax = priority_fee * assumed_gas * tax_multiple`\n14\t- **Rollup-Specific**: Only deployable to rollups adhering to priority fee ordering\n15\t\n16\t## Key Commands\n17\t\n18\t```bash\n19\tforge test          # Run all tests (Must be run and fully pass to claim a feature is complete)\n20\tforge test -vvv     # Verbose output & traces for debugging when tests fail\n21\tforge test --match-test <name>  # During development: Run specific tests to speedup iteration\n22\tforge test --match-contract <name> # During development: Run specific set of tests to speedup iteration\n23\t\n24\tforge fmt                           # Regularly format to make sure code is clean\n25\tforge lint                          # Run regularly to ensure stuff is clean\n26\t```\n27\t\n28\t## Angstrom L2 Implementation\n29\t\n30\t### Core Contract (`src/AngstromL2.sol`)\n31\t- Implements `IBeforeSwapHook` and `IAfterSwapHook` from Uniswap V4\n32\t- Inherits from `UniConsumer` for Uniswap integration\n33\t- Hook permissions configured for MEV tax collection\n34\t\n35\t### MEV Tax Mechanism\n36\t```solidity\n37\tSWAP_TAXED_GAS = 100_000        // Fixed gas estimate for swaps\n38\tSWAP_MEV_TAX_FACTOR = 49        // Tax rate = 49/50 = 98%\n39\ttax = priority_fee * SWAP_TAXED_GAS * SWAP_MEV_TAX_FACTOR\n40\t```\n41\t\n42\t### Key Functions\n43\t- `beforeSwap()`: Calculates and charges MEV tax on first swap of block\n44\t- `afterSwap()`: Distributes collected tax to LPs\n45\t- `_getSwapTaxAmount()`: Calculates tax based on priority fee\n46\t\n47\t\n48\t## Reusable Components from Original Angstrom\n49\t### Modules (`src/modules/`)\n50\t- **UniConsumer.sol**: Base Uniswap V4 integration (already used)\n51\t\n52\t### Libraries (`src/libraries/`)\n53\t- **TickLib.sol**: Tick math utilities\n54\t- **X128MathLib.sol**: Fixed-point math for rewards\n55\t\n56\t### Interfaces (`src/interfaces/`)\n57\t- **IUniV4.sol**: Library for efficiently retrieving state from Uniswap's `PoolManager`. `PoolManager` doesn't have view methods so this library provides a local interface that automatically computes the relevant storage slots, dispatches to `extsload` / `exttload` methods and decodes to the appropriate types. Used by importing and then adding a `using IUniV4 for (UniV4Inspector/IPoolManager);` declaration to the library or contract\n58\t\n59\t## Workflow for Implementing New Features and Components\n60\t1. Think about how the new feature is going to be used and plan out appropriate tests\n61\t2. Write appropriate unit tests, make sure to test under high fidelity conditions\n62\t3. Define any external interface necessary to be able to compile the tests\n63\t4. Run the new tests, they should fail\n64\t5. Implement the actual feature, progressively run the tests to ensure they pass & everything compiles\n65\t6.
